# Consistency audit: Neatoo user docs and sample sources against settled points S1-S13

Date 2026-10-05. Repo `C:\Users\KeithVoels\source\repos\neatoodotnet\Neatoo\` at Neatoo 0.35.0 (`Directory.Build.props:16`), RemoteFactory pinned 1.6.1 (`Directory.Packages.props:30-31`). Read-only: no repo file was edited, nothing was built, no test was run.

## 0. Method and caveats

- Scope: `docs/index.md`, `docs/getting-started.md`, `docs/guides/*`, `docs/reference/*`, `docs/vsCSLA/*`; every hand-written `.cs` in `src/samples/` (30 files); the four `.cs` files in `src/Neatoo.UnitTest.Demo/`. 52 files, 23,234 lines.
- The files were split into seven partitions (A-G). Each partition was read whole by a separate sub-agent working from the same brief and the same classification conventions, which wrote the row tables in section 10. The merge (this document's sections 1-9, 12, 13) was done afterwards: class normalization, duplicate marking, cross-partition contradictions, the S9 class count and the snippet publication checks. About 40 quoted lines behind the most consequential rows were re-read at merge and matched.
- Every behavioural statement about the framework comes from reading source (`src/Neatoo/`, the generators, checked-in generator output under `src/samples/Generated/`), never from execution. Rows that rest on reasoning the forks themselves flagged as unconfirmed: A-9, B-49, B-51, E-27, F-40, G-1.
- A doc code block between `<!-- snippet: X -->` and `<!-- endSnippet -->` is a copy of a `#region` in `src/samples`. The doc partitions reported findings inside such blocks as "snippet copy (region X)"; the sample partitions reported the same code at its source. Column 7 of each table in section 10 marks those doc rows as duplicates. The edit site for a snippet copy is the sample source; MarkdownSnippets regenerates the copies.
- RemoteFactory attribute declarations quoted in `docs/reference/api.md` were not verified (the types live in the NuGet package). Statements about RemoteFactory 1.9+ behaviour were checked against the local RemoteFactory checkout (`RemoteFactory/src/Directory.Build.props:19` says 1.10.0), not against the pinned 1.6.1 package.

## 2. Corrections and normalizations applied at merge

1. **A-7 withdrawn.** The fork proposed making the root `[Remote, Fetch] internal` method `public`. RemoteFactory's NF0105 is "`[Remote]` cannot be used with public methods ... `[Remote]` methods must be internal" (`RemoteFactory/src/Generator/DiagnosticDescriptors.cs:50-59`; the rule flipped in RemoteFactory v0.21.0, `RemoteFactory/docs/release-notes/v0.21.0.md:16-31`). All 14 `[Remote]` methods in `src/samples` are already `internal` (grep). Consequence for every S1-omission row (A-6, C-4, D-9, E-3, F-7, G-14): the fix is `[Remote]` plus `internal`, not `[Remote]` on a `public` method.
2. **Root operations without `[Remote]`** were classed CONTRADICTS S1 by forks A, C, D and UNRULED by forks E, F, G. Normalized to CONTRADICTS S1 (omission), following the caller's known example (BaseClassSamples `public [Insert]`, S1).
3. **Rules that inject a service interface** were classed CONTRADICTS S6 by forks B, D, G and UNRULED by forks E, F. Normalized to CONTRADICTS S6 (shape). The samples only ever register mocks in a `NeatooFactory.Logical` container (`src/samples/SamplesTestBase.cs:53-54, 65-69, 87`), so "server-only" is asserted by the surrounding prose ("email uniqueness requires a database query", validation.md:189), not by registration.
4. **Root `[Fetch]` whose parameters are the row data** was classed CONTRADICTS S2 by C, E, G and UNRULED by A, D, F. Normalized to CONTRADICTS S2 (weak) for root sites; child Fetch taking row values stays UNRULED.
5. **Rule classes typed on the concrete entity** normalized to UNRULED (S9-adjacent): S9's letter covers parameters, but `src/Design/Design.Domain/Rules/RuleBasics.cs:110` types rules on the concrete and makes the rule class `internal`.
6. **Rule-less ValidateBase demo classes** normalized to UNRULED (S8-adjacent) unless the class is labelled read model, DTO, summary or search criteria.
7. **Helper-file gap.** `src/samples/PropertiesSamples.cs:212` and `:767` mark regions with `// begin-snippet:` instead of `#region`; both are published (docs/guides/properties.md:457, :492) and were audited by forks B and F.

Reading of the numbers:

- **Interface-first (public interface + `internal` concrete): 2 of 194**, both in `SharedRulesSamples.cs` (`SharedRuleEmployee`, `SharedRuleProduct`), and even those two standalone entities' interfaces extend `IEntityBase`, not `IEntityRoot` (row G-18).
- **Public with no interface: 152** (EntityBase 70, ValidateBase 77, lists 5), in 25 of the 27 files that declare such classes (all but `LazyLoadSamples.cs` and `SharedRulesSamples.cs`).
- **Public concrete that does have an interface: 40** (child entities, child lists and validate items, plus one root). Still a violation of "the concrete class is `internal`".
- Exactly one user interface in all of `src/samples` extends `IEntityRoot`: `ISkillLazyParent` (`LazyLoadSamples.cs:19`). No other aggregate root has an interface.
- Three lists are parameterized on a concrete class: `BaseClassSamples.cs:270`, `CollectionsSamples.cs:44`, `EntitiesSamples.cs:279`.
- Files with no such classes: `SamplesTestBase.cs`, `SkillTestBase.cs`, `TestingPatternsTests.cs`.
- Full per-class list: scratchpad `s9-count.txt` ("All rows").

## 5. Cross-file contradictions found at merge

Each row is INTERNAL across partitions (two files in scope say opposite things). The right-hand side is the one that matches the settled list or the framework source.

| id | topic | says one thing | says the other (matches ruling / source) | rows |
|---|---|---|---|---|

## 6. Cascade shapes (consolidated)

Only one sample in all of `src/samples` implements a save cascade, and it is parent-iterates. No sample uses list-iterates. No published doc page contains cascade code.

| sample (file:lines) | region -> published at | who iterates the children | what is called on the child | what the child receives | deleted items |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `EntitiesCascadeOrder.InsertAsync` (src/samples/EntitiesSamples.cs:312-325) | `entities-cascade-insert` -> skills/neatoo/references/entities.md:463 | the PARENT (`for` loop in the root `[Insert]`) | `Items[i] = await itemFactory.SaveAsync(Items[i], Id)` (child's own `[Insert]`); result written back into the list slot | parent id, plus its own `[Service]` repository; child maps and writes its own row | n/a |
| `EntitiesCascadeOrder.UpdateAsync` (src/samples/EntitiesSamples.cs:329-355) | `entities-cascade-update` -> skills/neatoo/references/entities.md:510 | the PARENT, which also routes on child state (`if (Items[i].IsNew)` / `else if (Items[i].IsModified)`) | `SaveAsync(Items[i], Id)` for new, `SaveAsync(Items[i])` for modified | parent id for new; nothing for modified or deleted | the PARENT iterates `Items.Deleted` (a public passthrough of the protected `DeletedList`, EntitiesSamples.cs:282) and calls `itemFactory.SaveAsync(deleted)`. The list is `new`-ed in the constructor and has no factory operation, so `EntityListBase.FactoryComplete(Update)` never runs and DeletedList is never cleared (E-27, by source reading; no test runs this method) |
| `EntitiesCascadeOrder.DeleteAsync` (src/samples/EntitiesSamples.cs:358-362) | no region | nobody | nothing | nothing | children not cascaded |
| external caller `CascadeSave_OnlyRootSavedExternally` (src/samples/EntitiesSamples.cs:791-812) | `entities-cascade-correct-external` -> skills/neatoo/references/entities.md:548 | n/a | external code saves only the root (consistent with S4); the child is still created by the test through the child factory | n/a | n/a |
| `SkillEmployee` / `SkillEmployeeAddress` (src/samples/BaseClassSamples.cs:60-128, 148-197) | `edit-base-sample`, `editable-list-base-sample` -> skills/neatoo/references/base-classes.md:80, :171 | nobody; root Insert/Update/Delete each call `repository.XAsync(this)` | nothing; the child has only public `[Create]`/`[Fetch]` | nothing | only surfaced as `DeletedCount` |
| `SkillRfIntegrationRoot` / `SkillRfIntegrationChild` (src/samples/RemoteFactoryIntegrationSamples.cs:28-166) | `remote-factory-child-no-remote` -> docs/guides/remote-factory.md:116; `remote-factory-deletedlist-lifecycle` -> :158 | nobody; root Insert/Update write only the root row | nothing is called; child declares `public void Insert()`, `Update()`, `Delete()` with bodies `/* Persist through aggregate root */` | nothing (no parent id, no service) | not handled; comments claim "[Delete] called for each DeletedList item" and "DeletedList is cleared" |
| `Customer` / `Order` (src/samples/ReadmeSamples.cs:89-131) | `readme-quick-start` -> README.md:139 | nobody; root `[Remote][Insert] internal` body is `// Persistence logic here` | nothing; child has only `[Create]` | nothing | no; comment says "automatic parent cascade" |
| FETCH only: `SkillFactoryOrderWithItems` (src/samples/FactorySamples.cs:470-546), `RfOrder` (src/samples/RemoteFactorySamples.cs:440-531) | no region / `remotefactory-child-factories` (unpublished) | the PARENT's `[Fetch]` iterates repository rows | `itemFactory.Fetch(...)` (the child's own `[Fetch]`, consistent with S2), then `Items.Add(item)` | scalar row values, no parent id | n/a |

## 7. `[Execute]` declarations and sentences that depend on the pre-1.9 behaviour (consolidated)

A grep of all 52 files for `[Execute` finds five hits: one declaration, three comments beside it, and one doc table cell.

| file:line | declaration / sentence | `[Remote]` | `private static` + underscore | published at | row |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| src/samples/BaseClassSamples.cs:215-220 | `[Execute]` `internal static async Task<bool> _SendEmail(string to, string subject, string body, [Service] ISkillEmailService emailService)` in `[Factory] public static partial class SkillSendEmailCommand` | no | underscore yes, but `internal`, not `private` | region `command-base-sample` -> skills/neatoo/references/base-classes.md:199 | D-1 |
| src/samples/BaseClassSamples.cs:207-208 | "Commands are static classes with [Execute] methods that generate delegates. The delegate is injected via DI and always executes on the server." | - | - | same region | D-2 |
| src/samples/BaseClassSamples.cs:201 | "Command Pattern - Static classes with [Execute] methods for server operations" | - | - | no region | D-2 |
| src/samples/BaseClassSamples.cs:213 | "// [Execute] generates a delegate: SendEmailCommand.SendEmail" (class is `SkillSendEmailCommand`) | - | - | same region | D-3 |
| docs/vsCSLA/concept-map.md:15 | "`CommandBase<T>` \| Static class with `[Factory]` + `[Execute]` \| No base class needed" | - | - | prose | C-27 |
| docs/vsCSLA/concept-map.md:36 | "Inverse: Neatoo methods are local unless marked `[Remote]`" (true of `[Execute]` only on 1.9+) | - | - | prose | C-27 |
| docs/reference/api.md (whole file) | no entry for `[Execute]` or `[Remote]` although line 5 says "Complete reference" | - | - | prose | C-5 |

Why the sample works today: under the pinned 1.6.1 the generated client registration routes the delegate through `IMakeRemoteDelegateRequest.ForDelegate` (`src/samples/Generated/Neatoo.Generator/Neatoo.Factory/Samples.SkillSendEmailCommandFactory.g.cs:23-25`). On 1.9+ a bare `[Execute]` has no remote delegate, and `internal static` without `[Remote]` is server-only (`RemoteFactory/src/RemoteFactory/FactoryAttributes.cs:117-131`).

Gap, not a row: no audited file shows the S6 shape (a rule taking an injected `[Execute]` command), and no audited doc page explains commands at all.

## 8. UNRULED register

### 8.1 Merge-level items (not in any fork table)

| id | location | verbatim | why it needs a ruling |
|---|---|---|---|
| MU-1 | S1 itself versus src/Design/Design.Domain/Aggregates/OrderAggregate/OrderItem.cs:52-59 and OrderItemList.cs:35-36; RemoteFactory/src/RemoteFactory/FactoryAttributes.cs:129-131; skills/neatoo/references/entities.md:734 | Design: `[Create]` / `public void Create()` on the child and on the child list, with `[Fetch]`, `[Insert]`, `[Update]` `internal`. RemoteFactory: "`internal static` without `[Remote]` is server-only like every other internal operation — guarded, trimmable". Skill: "(ensured by keeping `[Create]` public on the child)" | S1 says "Child entity operations are `internal`". If that includes `[Create]`, an `internal` child Create is server-only and the client cannot create a child locally. If `[Create]` is the exception, the `[Create]` half of patterns P03 and P04 is not a contradiction and only the Fetch/Insert/Update/Delete sites are. The fix column of rows D-10, D-11, E-1, E-2, F-4, F-6, G-13, A-8, A-30, C-3 depends on this. |
| MU-2 | skills/neatoo/references/entities.md:733 and :729 (outside this audit's files) | "`[Remote]` + `internal` triggers NF0105 diagnostic error. Remove `[Remote]` before making a method `internal`." / "must keep all factory methods `public` with `[Remote]`" | Reverse of the current diagnostic (see section 2 item 1). Reported because it produced the withdrawn row A-7 and conflicts with docs/guides/remote-factory.md:50. |

Path conventions per partition: A and C and G rows are repo-relative; B rows are under `docs/guides/`; D, E, F rows with a bare file name are under `src/samples/`.

| F-39 | P25 | Rule-less `ValidateBase` demo classes: PropertiesSamples.cs:18, :39; QuickStartSamples.cs:31; PropertySamples.cs:21, :46, :78 (6) | `public partial class PropEmployee : ValidateBase<PropEmployee>` (XML summary: "Entity demonstrating partial property declaration.") | UNRULED (S8-adjacent: "ValidateBase only when the object needs rules"; none is labelled a read model, so not classed as a contradiction) |
| G-3 | P33 | src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:762-763 | `// Property is not marked as invalid until rules run` / `// (In real usage, factory method would call RunRules after loading)` | UNRULED (S3/S10-adjacent): tells the reader a factory method should end with `RunRules`. Settled list says a factory load runs no rules and `RunRules` is a forced re-run, but does not say whether a `[Fetch]` should validate what it loaded. |
| G-4 | P15 | 2 call sites: src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:756-757; src/samples/TestingPatternsTests.cs:525 (comments :516, :524) | `invoice["Amount"].LoadValue(-100m); // Would fail validation rule` / `// LoadValue bypasses validation` | UNRULED (S3-adjacent): external code calls `LoadValue` through the indexer on a live object to plant a rule-violating value. With S3 removing `LoadValue` from factory bodies, the samples now show no sanctioned use of it, only this one. |
| G-5 | P10 | 2 sites: src/samples/SkillValidationSamples.cs:323; src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:352 | `LineItemsProperty.LoadValue(new SkillValidLineItemList());` / `LineItemsProperty.LoadValue(new ValidationLineItemList());` | UNRULED (S3-adjacent: constructor, not a factory operation; S9-adjacent: `new` on the concrete list). List is created in the parent's constructor in both. Same shape as the known BaseClassSamples example. |
| G-6 | P11 | 2 sites: src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:725-741; src/samples/TestingPatternsTests.cs:584-604 | `// Pause validation during batch updates` / `// - To run rules after batch updates: await order.RunRules(RunRulesFlag.All);` | UNRULED: S3 bans `PauseAllActions` inside a factory operation only; these teach outside (UI/test) code to pause a live object. The behavioural comments at :737-741 are accurate (ResumeAllActions runs no rules: ValidateBase.cs:798-806; PropertyChanged is guarded by IsPaused: ValidateBase.cs:356-361). |
| G-7 | P13 | src/samples/TestingPatternsTests.cs:339-341; src/samples/SourceGenerationSamples.cs:97-98 | `order.DoMarkUnmodified(); // Simulate loaded from DB` / `public void DoMarkNew() => MarkNew();` `public void DoMarkOld() => MarkOld();` | UNRULED (S3-adjacent): protected state markers exposed as public methods and driven from outside a factory operation. The "simulate" comment is also inaccurate against source: an un-paused `Add` marks the fetched item modified (EntityListBase.cs:283) and `MarkUnmodified` clears only the order's own properties (EntityBase.cs:306-318), so the graph is not in a loaded state. `DoMarkNew`/`DoMarkOld` on `SkillGenSaveEntity` have no callers in `src/samples` (grep). |
| G-16 | P28 | 12 sites (type argument + `Execute` parameter): src/samples/SkillGapSamples.cs:19, 30, 48, 54; src/samples/SkillValidationSamples.cs:141, 152, 202, 207; src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:113, 117, 366, 370 | `public class SkillGapUniqueEmailRule : AsyncRuleBase<SkillGapEmployee>` / `SkillGapEmployee target, CancellationToken? token = null)` | UNRULED (S9-adjacent: rule target type). Normalized to match B-20, E-21. By S9's letter a concrete-typed `Execute(Concrete target)` parameter is a violation, but Design.Domain types rules on the concrete and makes the rule `internal`, so this needs a ruling. |
| G-19 | P25 | src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:17-28 (and test comment :489) | `public partial class ValidationCustomer : ValidateBase<ValidationCustomer>` / `// No validation rules, so valid` | UNRULED (S8-adjacent). Normalized from 'CONTRADICTS S8 (weak)' to match D-42, F-39: the class is not labelled a read model. |
| G-20 | P19 | src/samples/ValidationSamples.cs:301-307, 703-704 | `public void MarkTransactionInvalid(string reason)` / `transaction.MarkTransactionInvalid("Transaction rejected by payment gateway");` | UNRULED (S5-adjacent): validity pushed into the object imperatively by outside code via `MarkInvalid`, not by a rule. Framework XML doc endorses `MarkInvalid` for "object-level validation that cannot be expressed as a property rule" (ValidateBase.cs:632-633) and notes `RunRules(All)` clears it (:628-629). |

### Part D - samples: ApiReferenceSamples.cs, AsyncSamples.cs, AuthorizationSamples.cs, BaseClassSamples.cs, BlazorSamples.cs

Paths: bare sample file names are under `src/samples/`; `api.md` = docs/reference/api.md, `async.md` / `blazor.md` = docs/guides/, `skill X.md` = skills/neatoo/references/X.md.

Framework facts used for verification (all read in `src\Neatoo\`, nothing built or run):
- `EntityBase.cs:180` `IsModified => PropertyManager.IsModified || IsDeleted || IsSelfModified` (no IsNew term)
- `EntityBase.cs:205` `IsSavable => (IsModified || IsNew) && IsValid && !IsBusy` (no child/aggregate term)

- `EntityBase.cs:476-503` `Save()` throws NotModified only when `!(IsModified || IsNew)`
- `EntityBase.cs:594-625` `FactoryComplete`: Create -> MarkNew; Fetch and Delete -> nothing; Insert/Update -> MarkUnmodified + MarkOld
- `EntityBase.cs:306-318` `MarkUnmodified` clears self only (`PropertyManager.MarkSelfUnmodified()`)
- `EntityBase.cs:389` `public void Delete()` (non-virtual)
- `EntityListBase.cs:217-317` live `InsertItem` calls `MarkModified()` on EVERY item (new or existing); paused adds do not
- `EntityListBase.cs:538-571` DeletedList is cleared only in the LIST's own `FactoryComplete(FactoryOperation.Update)`; the only `SetContainingList(null)` in src\Neatoo is line 546
- `ValidateBase.cs:354-360` PropertyChanged is dropped (not queued) while paused; `ValidateBase.cs:798-806` ResumeAllActions fires nothing, runs no rules
- `ValidateBase.cs:534-563` OnDeserialized re-hooks events/parents and resumes; runs no rules
- `RemoteFactory/Internal/NeatooBaseJsonTypeConverter.cs:301-302` rule messages ARE serialized (`SerializedRuleMessages`)
- Generated `src/samples/Generated/Neatoo.Generator/Neatoo.Factory/Samples.EntitiesCustomerFactory.g.cs:346-355`: Save checks `IsDeleted` first (and short-circuits when also `IsNew`), then `IsNew`, else Update

##### Claims ledger

What `[Remote]` means / where code runs
- CT:5 "Change tracking runs on the client — in Blazor, WPF, or any data-bound UI — not on the server."
- CT:5 "when the aggregate reaches the server for persistence, the factory methods need to know *exactly* what changed"
- RF:5 "Neatoo entities integrate with RemoteFactory for factory generation and client-server execution."
- RF:50-51 `[Remote, Fetch] internal async Task Fetch(...)` (root)
- RF:119 "// Child entities do NOT use [Remote] - they persist through the aggregate root"
- RF:133 "// Insert/Update/Delete called by parent's Save() - no [Remote] needed"
- GS:241, GS:259 "`NeatooFactory.Logical` option means all operations run locally (no client-server split)"
- No sentence in the 8 files says what `[Remote]` means; GS and ENT root samples omit it entirely.

LoadValue / PauseAllActions / MarkUnmodified
- COL:11 "Initialize child collections in entity constructors using `LoadValue()` to establish the parent-child relationship without triggering modification tracking."
- RF:55 "// - Property assignments use LoadValue semantics (no IsModified change)" (inside `[Fetch]`)
- RF:54 "// - IsPaused = true (validation and modification tracking suspended)"
- CT:452 "During factory operations (Create, Fetch, Insert, Update, Delete), Neatoo pauses tracking so that setting properties from persistence data doesn't trigger rules, fire events, or mark the entity as modified."
- CT:452 "The same mechanism is available to developers for batch operations — loading from a DTO, deserializing, or bulk updates"
- CT:490-494 "Use pausing when: Loading data from persistence ... Deserializing from JSON ... bulk property updates ... Initializing computed or derived properties"
- CT:496 "Neatoo automatically pauses during all factory operations (Create, Fetch, Insert, Update, Delete)"
- CT:483-488 "While paused ... Validation rules do not execute - `PropertyChanged` events are not raised"
- PC:511-522 "During deserialization and factory operations, the framework pauses parent cascade"; "Property change events are deferred"; "Property change events fire for any accumulated changes"
- COL:434-448 "Collections respect the IsPaused flag during deserialization and factory operations"; "Factory operations (FactoryStart, before data loading begins)"
- ENT:513 "`MarkUnmodified()` is protected and called internally by `FactoryComplete` after successful Insert or Update operations. Users do not call it directly."
- CT:97, CT:128 "The framework calls this automatically after Insert and Update operations complete."
- CT:130 "You cannot mark an entity as unmodified while async operations are in progress. Call `await WaitForTasks()` first."
- COL:155 "`MarkUnmodified()` is called automatically by the framework after Fetch or successful save operations."
- ENT:161 "`FactoryComplete(Fetch)` performs no state changes"
- GI:16 "MarkModified/MarkUnmodified"; GI:28 "LoadValue vs direct assignment"; GI:31 "PauseAllActions for batching"; GS:294 "Property system internals, LoadValue, meta-properties"

- CT:162-166 "Use `MarkModified` when: A timestamp property should be updated on every save ... version number ... computed columns ... External state changes"

Who saves children and how

None. There is no `[Execute]` and no `[Remote]` attribute in the nine files (the `Execute(...)` overrides are rule methods). No comment assumes Execute is remote.

Wiring facts from the two test bases, relevant to S6/S7:
- Both containers are built with `services.AddNeatooServices(NeatooFactory.Logical, <assembly>)` (SamplesTestBase.cs:54, SkillTestBase.cs:53); neither registers a command delegate by hand, so any `[Execute]` delegate in other files reaches DI only through that call.
- Rules that need a lookup are wired to plain services by hand: `ProductAvailabilityRule(IInventoryService)` :92-93, `UniqueEmailRule(IEmailValidationService)` :94-95, `EarlyExitRule(IInventoryService)` :103-104, `CancellableValidationRule(IPricingService)` :105-106, `IIdUniquenessRule -> IdUniquenessRule` :119 (all SamplesTestBase.cs). The rule classes are in other forks' files.
- Repositories registered in the same container as the rule services (SamplesTestBase.cs:72, 75-76, 79, 82-84, 88, 122-134). `IContactRepository` is registered under "Async samples" (:88) next to the async rule service; its consumer is outside this partition and worth a look for S6.

## 12. Coverage summary

All 52 files in scope were read whole by the partition that owned them (per-file tables in section 11); no file was left unfinished.

- Part A (8 files, 2,928 lines): docs/index.md, docs/getting-started.md, docs/guides/index.md, entities.md, parent-child.md, collections.md, change-tracking.md, remote-factory.md.
- Part B (5 files, 3,522 lines): docs/guides/async.md, blazor.md, business-rules.md, properties.md, validation.md.

None of the four Demo files reads as a how-to (scratch test, generator smoke test, empty types, generator driver), so they produced no rows.

- Part E (6 files, 3,513 lines): BusinessRulesSamples.cs, ChangeTrackingSamples.cs, CollectionSamples.cs, CollectionsSamples.cs, EntitiesSamples.cs, EntitySamples.cs.

- Part G (9 files, 3,217 lines): SamplesTestBase.cs, SharedRulesSamples.cs, SkillBlazorSamples.cs, SkillGapSamples.cs, SkillTestBase.cs, SkillValidationSamples.cs, SourceGenerationSamples.cs, TestingPatternsTests.cs, ValidationSamples.cs.
